Current route status
- route.ts has production_static_preview.
- route.ts still requires x-controlled-intake-no-write-preview.
- route.ts still has productionWriteEnabled=false.
- route.ts still has dbReadExecuted=false.
- route.ts still has dbWriteExecuted=false.

- route.ts still has aiCallExecuted=false.
- legacy no_write_preview still works.
- production_static_preview still uses explicit preview headers only for no-write verification.
- no production write route exists.

Server-side app user mapping design target
Future server-side mapping must convert authenticated server identity into stable application user context:
- auth subject / provider identity
- stable appUserId / internal user_id
- optional actorId
- optional organizationId
- optional spaceId

Forbidden mapping sources
- request body user_id
- request body appUserId
- request body actorId
- request body organizationId
- request body spaceId
- client-controlled ownership fields
- production preview headers in production write mode

Allowed future mapping sources
- server-side Auth0/session identity
- existing user sync/profile mapping
- server-side Supabase read after explicit read gate
- server-verified actor/organization/space membership after explicit read gate

Required fail-closed behavior
- missing session identity -> fail
- missing auth subject -> fail
- missing user mapping -> fail
- ambiguous user mapping -> fail
- actor/organization/space not verified -> fail
- any client ownership field in body -> fail

Required future failure codes
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_SERVER_SESSION_REQUIRED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_SUBJECT_REQUIRED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_APP_USER_MAPPING_REQUIRED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_APP_USER_MAPPING_AMBIGUOUS
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_CONTEXT_NOT_VERIFIED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_ACTOR_NOT_ALLOWED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_ORGANIZATION_NOT_ALLOWED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_SPACE_NOT_ALLOWED

Recommended next O-AK scope
O-AK should create a server-side app user mapping contract document only.
It should not perform DB reads.
It should not implement Supabase resolver yet.
It should define:
- exact input shape from server session
- exact read model required later
- expected successful mapping shape
- expected failure shape
- membership/context verification boundaries
- RLS/security assumptions to verify later
- logging/audit boundaries
- no-write guardrails

Recommended future file after O-AK/O-AL
Potential future helper:
- lib/activity/controlledIntake/serverSideAppUserMapping.ts

Future helper should be created only after O-AK contract and O-AL verification.
The first implementation should still be static/no-I/O or injected-read-result based.
Actual Supabase read should require a separate explicit read gate.
